(function()
 
pcall(function()
    local previous = _G.__S11AutoGrabIntegratedEngine
    if type(previous) == "table" and type(previous.Destroy) == "function" then previous.Destroy() end
    _G.__S11AutoGrabIntegratedEngine = nil
end)
_G.__S11GetAutoGrabIntegrated = function()
local old = _G.__S11AutoGrabIntegratedEngine
if type(old) == "table" and type(old.GetStatus) == "function" then
    local ok, st = pcall(old.GetStatus)
    if ok and type(st) == "table" and not st.destroyed and st.engineVersion == "ZURICH_V3_ORIGINAL_100_75" then return old end
    if type(old.Destroy) == "function" then pcall(old.Destroy) end
end
local source = [======[
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
if not Player then error("S11: Zurich V3 requires a client") end
local plots = workspace:WaitForChild("Plots")
local autoStealValues = { Radius = 65 }
local CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL = {
    AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED = false,
    HOLD_MIN = 1.3, HOLD_MAX = 2.6,
    ENTRY_DELAY = 0.3, COOLDOWN = 0.05,
    STEAL_RANGE = 9, PRIME_RANGE = 80,
}
local StealState = {
    active = false, startTime = 0, phase = "idle", label = "",
    lastResult = "", lastResultTime = 0, totalSteals = 0, failedSteals = 0,
}
local State = { isStealing = false }
local function setStealState(on)
    State.isStealing = on == true
end
local autoStealGeneration = 0
local v1Progress = 0
_G.__ZurichAutoStealV3Variant = "100"
            -- Auto Grab V3: mismo flujo de hold/trigger del código proporcionado.
            local V3 = { busy = false, data = {}, halfFireRange = 10, halfHoldMin = 1.3, halfHoldMax = 2.6, halfEntryDelay = 0.3 }

            local function v3PromptDistance(prompt)
            local char = Player.Character
            local root = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
            if not root then return math.huge end
            local part = prompt.Parent
            if part and part:IsA("Attachment") then part = part.Parent end
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude end
            local ok, position = pcall(function() return prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent.WorldPosition end)
            return ok and position and (position - root.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
            end

            local function v3FindNearestPrompt()
            local char = Player.Character
            local root = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
            if not root then return nil end
            local nearest, nearestDistance, radius = nil, math.huge, (autoStealValues.Radius or 55)
            for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local yourBase = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
            if plot:IsA("Model") and not (yourBase and yourBase:IsA("BillboardGui") and yourBase.Enabled) then
            local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if pods then for _, pod in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
            local spawn = pod:FindFirstChild("Base") and pod.Base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
            local distance = spawn and (spawn.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
            if distance and distance <= radius and distance < nearestDistance then
            local found = nil
            for _, candidate in ipairs(spawn:GetDescendants()) do
            if candidate:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and candidate.ActionText and candidate.ActionText:find("Steal") then found = candidate end
            end
            if found then nearest, nearestDistance = found, distance end
            end
            end end
            end
            end
            return nearest
            end

            local function v3ExecuteSteal(prompt)
            if V3.busy then return end
            local data = V3.data[prompt]
            if not data then
            data = { hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true }; V3.data[prompt] = data
            if getconnections then
            for _, connection in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do if connection.Function then table.insert(data.hold, connection.Function) end end
            for _, connection in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do if connection.Function then table.insert(data.trigger, connection.Function) end end
            end
            end
            if not data.ready then return end
            data.ready, V3.busy, StealState.active, StealState.startTime, StealState.phase = false, true, true, tick(), "holding"
            v1Progress = 0
            setStealState(true)
            local runGeneration = autoStealGeneration
            task.spawn(function()
            if _G.__ZurichAutoStealV3Variant == "75" then
            local cap, duration, rangeWait, triggerDistance = 0.75, 1.4, 2.2, 10
            for _, callback in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(callback) end
            local startTime = tick()
            while runGeneration == autoStealGeneration and CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED and tick() - startTime < duration do
            v1Progress = math.min((tick() - startTime) / duration, cap)
            if v1Progress >= cap then break end
            task.wait()
            end
            local waitStart, completed = tick(), false
            while runGeneration == autoStealGeneration and CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED and tick() - waitStart < rangeWait do
            v1Progress = cap
            if v3PromptDistance(prompt) <= triggerDistance then completed = true break end
            task.wait()
            end
            if completed and runGeneration == autoStealGeneration and CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED then
            local finishStart = tick()
            while tick() - finishStart < 0.35 do
            v1Progress = cap + math.min((tick() - finishStart) / 0.35, 1) * (1 - cap)
            task.wait()
            end
            v1Progress = 1
            for _, callback in ipairs(data.trigger) do task.spawn(callback) end
            StealState.totalSteals = StealState.totalSteals + 1; StealState.lastResult = "Stole"
            end
            StealState.active, StealState.phase, StealState.lastResultTime = false, "idle", tick()
            setStealState(false); task.wait(0.15); v1Progress = 0
            data.ready, V3.busy = true, false
            return
            end
            for _, callback in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(callback) end
            task.spawn(function()
            while V3.busy and StealState.active and tick() - StealState.startTime < V3.halfHoldMin do
            v1Progress = math.clamp((tick() - StealState.startTime) / V3.halfHoldMin, 0, 1)
            task.wait()
            end
            end)
            task.wait(V3.halfHoldMin)
            if runGeneration ~= autoStealGeneration then return end
            local wasInRange = v3PromptDistance(prompt) <= V3.halfFireRange
            while runGeneration == autoStealGeneration and tick() - StealState.startTime <= V3.halfHoldMax and prompt.Parent and CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED do
            v1Progress = math.clamp((tick() - StealState.startTime) / math.max(V3.halfHoldMin, 0.01), 0, 1)
            if v3PromptDistance(prompt) <= V3.halfFireRange then
            if not wasInRange then task.wait(V3.halfEntryDelay) end
            for _, callback in ipairs(data.trigger) do task.spawn(callback) end
            StealState.totalSteals = StealState.totalSteals + 1; StealState.lastResult = "Stole"
            break
            end
            task.wait()
            end
            if runGeneration ~= autoStealGeneration then return end
            StealState.active, StealState.phase, StealState.lastResultTime = false, "idle", tick()
            v1Progress = 1
            setStealState(false); task.wait(0.05); v1Progress = 0
            task.wait(0.45); data.ready, V3.busy = true, false
            end)
            end


local stealConnection
local destroyed = false
local lastScanAt = 0
local characterReadyAfter = tick() + 2
local RAGDOLL_RESTART_PAUSE = 2.0
local ragdollRecoveryUntil = 0
local ragdollLatched = false
local watchedRagdollHumanoid = nil
local ragdollStateConn = nil
local ragdollPlatformConn = nil
local function characterIsUnready()
    local character = Player.Character
    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not character or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return true end
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root or tick() < characterReadyAfter then return true end
    return false
end
local function isRagdolled()
    local character = Player.Character
    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return false end
    if hum.PlatformStand then return true end
    local state = hum:GetState()
    return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
        or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
end
local function cancelInProgress()
    autoStealGeneration += 1
    V3.busy = false
    V3.data = {}
    StealState.active = false
    StealState.phase = "idle"
    setStealState(false)
    v1Progress = 0
    _G.__S11AutoGrabHudProgress = 0
    _G.__S11AutoGrabHudState = "IDLE"
end
local function refreshHud()
    _G.__S11AutoGrabHudProgress = v1Progress
    if StealState.active then
        _G.__S11AutoGrabHudState = (v1Progress >= 0.75 and _G.__ZurichAutoStealV3Variant == "75") and "WAITING" or "STEALING"
    else
        _G.__S11AutoGrabHudState = "IDLE"
    end
end
local function detectRagdollHit()
    if not CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED then return end
    local ragdolled = isRagdolled()
    if ragdolled and not ragdollLatched then
        ragdollLatched = true
        ragdollRecoveryUntil = tick() + RAGDOLL_RESTART_PAUSE
        if StealState.active or V3.busy then cancelInProgress() end
        _G.__S11AutoGrabRagdollBlocked = true
    elseif not ragdolled then
        ragdollLatched = false
    end
end
local function unbindRagdollSignals()
    if ragdollStateConn then ragdollStateConn:Disconnect(); ragdollStateConn = nil end
    if ragdollPlatformConn then ragdollPlatformConn:Disconnect(); ragdollPlatformConn = nil end
    watchedRagdollHumanoid = nil
    ragdollLatched = false
end
local function bindRagdollSignals()
    local char = Player.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum == watchedRagdollHumanoid then return end
    unbindRagdollSignals()
    if not hum then return end
    watchedRagdollHumanoid = hum
    ragdollStateConn = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function()
        if Player.Character == char then detectRagdollHit() end
    end)
    ragdollPlatformConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PlatformStand"):Connect(function()
        if Player.Character == char then detectRagdollHit() end
    end)
    detectRagdollHit()
end
local function _startStealHeartbeat()
    if stealConnection then return end
    stealConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        bindRagdollSignals()
        detectRagdollHit()
        local blocked = isRagdolled() or tick() < ragdollRecoveryUntil
        _G.__S11AutoGrabRagdollBlocked = blocked
        _G.__S11AutoGrabAttemptActive = StealState.active or V3.busy or blocked
        refreshHud()
        if blocked then
            _G.__S11AutoGrabHudProgress = 0
            _G.__S11AutoGrabHudState = "IDLE"
            return
        end
        if not CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED then return end
        if characterIsUnready() then return end
        if Player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return end
        if StealState.active or V3.busy then return end
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastScanAt < 0.10 then return end
        lastScanAt = now
        local ok, prompt = pcall(v3FindNearestPrompt)
        if not ok then return end
        if prompt then
            local success, err = pcall(v3ExecuteSteal, prompt)
            if not success then
                cancelInProgress()
                warn("[S11 AutoGrab Zurich V3] " .. tostring(err))
            end
        end
    end)
end
local Engine = {}
function Engine.Start()
    if destroyed then return false, "destroyed" end
    characterReadyAfter = math.max(characterReadyAfter, tick() + 0.35)
    if CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED then return true end
    CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED = true
    bindRagdollSignals()
    if type(_G.__S11SetAutoGrabAntiRagdoll) == "function" then
        pcall(_G.__S11SetAutoGrabAntiRagdoll, true)
    end
    _startStealHeartbeat()
    return true
end
function Engine.Stop(preserveRagdollPause)
    autoStealGeneration = autoStealGeneration + 1
    if stealConnection then stealConnection:Disconnect(); stealConnection = nil end
    unbindRagdollSignals()
    CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED = false
    if preserveRagdollPause ~= true then ragdollRecoveryUntil = 0 end
    _G.__S11AutoGrabRagdollBlocked = false
    _G.__S11AutoGrabAttemptActive = false
    if type(_G.__S11SetAutoGrabAntiRagdoll) == "function" then
        pcall(_G.__S11SetAutoGrabAntiRagdoll, false)
    end
    StealState.active = false
    StealState.phase = "idle"
    setStealState(false)
    v1Progress = 0
    V3.busy = false
    V3.data = {}
    _G.__S11AutoGrabHudProgress = 0
    _G.__S11AutoGrabHudState = "IDLE"
    return true
end
function Engine.SetMode(mode)
    if destroyed then return false, "destroyed" end
    mode = tostring(mode or "NORMAL"):upper()
    if mode ~= "NORMAL" and mode ~= "SEMI" and mode ~= "V1" and mode ~= "V2" then
        return false, "NORMAL/SEMI"
    end
    local variant = (mode == "SEMI" or mode == "V2") and "75" or "100"
    if _G.__ZurichAutoStealV3Variant ~= variant then
        local running = CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED
        if running then Engine.Stop(true) end
        _G.__ZurichAutoStealV3Variant = variant
        if running then Engine.Start() end
    end
    return true
end
function Engine.SetRadius(studs)
    studs = tonumber(studs)
    if not studs or studs < 1 or studs > 200 then return false, "Radius: 1-200" end
    autoStealValues.Radius = studs
    return true
end
function Engine.GetStatus()
    return {
        enabled = CONFIG_AUTO_STEAL.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED,
        mode = (_G.__ZurichAutoStealV3Variant == "75") and "SEMI" or "NORMAL",
        engineVersion = "ZURICH_V3_ORIGINAL_100_75",
        pausePercent = (_G.__ZurichAutoStealV3Variant == "75") and 75 or 100,
        radius = autoStealValues.Radius,
        active = StealState.active,
        phase = StealState.phase,
        progress = v1Progress,
        triggeredAttempts = StealState.totalSteals,
        failedAttempts = StealState.failedSteals,
        result = StealState.lastResult,
        destroyed = destroyed,
    }
end
function Engine.Destroy()
    if destroyed then return true end
    Engine.Stop()
    destroyed = true
    if _G.__S11AutoGrabIntegratedEngine == Engine then _G.__S11AutoGrabIntegratedEngine = nil end
    return true
end
_G.__S11AutoGrabIntegratedEngine = Engine
return Engine
]======]
local compiled, err = loadstring(source, "@S11_Duels_Zurich_AutoGrab_V3_Original")
if not compiled then
    warn("[S11 Zurich V3] Compile error: " .. tostring(err))
    return nil
end
local ok, engine = pcall(compiled)
if not ok or type(engine) ~= "table" then
    warn("[S11 Zurich V3] Initialization error: " .. tostring(engine))
    return nil
end
return engine
end

(function()
local S11_ASSET_IDS
local function __S11_AssetId(nombre)
local id = S11_ASSET_IDS[nombre]
if (id == nil or id == "") and type(_G) == "table" then
local external = rawget(_G, "S11_HUB_ASSET_IDS")
if type(external) == "table" then id = external[nombre] end
end
if type(id) == "number" and id > 0 and id == math.floor(id) then
id = string.format("%.0f", id)
end
if type(id) ~= "string" then return nil end
local numeric = id:match("^%s*(%d+)%s*$") or id:match("^rbxassetid://(%d+)$")
if numeric and #numeric >= 6 and tonumber(numeric) and tonumber(numeric) > 0 then
return "rbxassetid://" .. numeric
end
return nil
end

local ok, datos = pcall(readfile, archivo)
if ok and type(datos) == "string" and #datos == longitud
and datos:match("^[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$") then return datos end
end
warn("[Sunburn] Asset no disponible: " .. tostring(nombre)); return nil
end

if image and image.Image ~= "" then return image.Image end

end)

end

    local function enableAutoSteal()
        local getEngine = _G.__S11GetAutoGrabIntegrated
        local engine = type(getEngine) == "function" and getEngine() or nil
        if not engine then return false end
        engine.SetRadius(math.clamp(tonumber(autoStealValues.Radius) or 65, 1, 200))
        engine.SetMode(autoStealMode == "v2" and "SEMI" or "NORMAL")
        return engine.Start()
    end
    local function disableAutoSteal()
        local engine = _G.__S11AutoGrabIntegratedEngine
        if engine then pcall(engine.Stop) end
        _G.__S11AutoGrabHudProgress = 0
        _G.__S11AutoGrabHudState = "IDLE"
    end
    _G.S11AutoGrab = {
        GetMode = function() return autoStealMode end,
        SetMode = function(mode)
            if mode ~= "v1" and mode ~= "v2" then return false end
            autoStealMode = mode
            local engine = _G.__S11AutoGrabIntegratedEngine
            if engine then engine.SetMode(mode == "v2" and "SEMI" or "NORMAL") end
            saveConfig()
            return true
        end,
    }
    _G.__S11AutoGrabRagdollBlocked = false
    _G.__S11TrackConn(Player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
        disableAutoSteal()
    end))
    _G.__S11TrackConn(Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
        task.spawn(function()
            local humanoid = newCharacter:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            local root = newCharacter:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
            if not humanoid or not root then return end
            task.wait(0.8)
            if _G.__S11_STATE ~= s11RuntimeState or Player.Character ~= newCharacter then return end
            if toggleStates["auto steal"] ~= true then return end
            enableAutoSteal()
            local update = toggleVisualUpdaters["auto steal"]
            if update then pcall(update) end
        end)
    end))
    _G.__S11TrackStopper(disableAutoSteal)
    FeaturePostToggle["auto steal"] = function(active)
        if active then enableAutoSteal() else disableAutoSteal() end
    end
    if toggleStates["auto steal"] then enableAutoSteal() else disableAutoSteal() end
end

_,yawError=(root.CFrame:Inverse()*targetCFrame):ToEulerAnglesXYZ()bodyLock.AngularVelocity=root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0,math.clamp(yawError,-math.pi,math.pi)*100,0))end end)end local function heartbeatCounter()if not

or state==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll if ragdolled then lastRagdoll=true if not stateGuard then stateGuard=true startRotationCounter()end local bat=findBatForCounter()if bat then swingBatForCounter(bat,character)end else stateGuard=false
lastRagdoll=false end if refreshGUI then refreshGUI()end end local function setCounter(value)value=value==true if enabled==value then if refreshGUI then refreshGUI()end return end enabled=value if enabled then stateGuard=false heartbeatConnection=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(heartbeatCounter)else
if heartbeatConnection then heartbeatConnection:Disconnect()heartbeatConnection=nil end clearRotation()stateGuard=false lastRagdoll=false end if refreshGUI then refreshGUI()end end local charConn=_G.__S11TrackConn(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()clearRotation()stateGuard=false
lastRagdoll=false counterPauseUntil=0 end))return{start=function()setCounter(true)end,stop=function()setCounter(false)end,}end)()local function stopSelectedRagdollCounter()s11CounterV1.stop()stopRagdollCounter()end local function startSelectedRagdollCounter()stopSelectedRagdollCounter()if

then pcall(_G.__S11ClearEnemyRagdollTimer)end end _G.__S11TrackConn(Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()task.wait(0.5);wasRagdolled=false if toggleStates["Ragdoll Counter"]then startSelectedRagdollCounter()end end))if toggleStates["Ragdoll Counter"]then
startSelectedRagdollCounter()end _G.__S11TrackStopper(stopSelectedRagdollCounter)end do local MEDUSA_COOLDOWN=25 local medusaDebounce=false local medusaLastUsed=0 local medusaCounterEnabled=false local medusaConns={}local function findMedusa()local

end local function useMedusa()if not medusaCounterEnabled then return end if medusaDebounce then return end if tick()-medusaLastUsed<MEDUSA_COOLDOWN then return end local char=Player.Character if not char then return end medusaDebounce=true

startMedusaCounter()end end local s11ImportedV1Start,s11ImportedV1Stop
do
 
 
local speedValues = { CypherAimbotApproachSpeed = 55 }
local LP = Player
local function getBat()
local char = LP.Character
if char then
local equippedTool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
if equippedTool then
local equippedName = equippedTool.Name:lower()
if equippedName:find("bat", 1, true) or equippedName:find("slap", 1, true) then
return equippedTool
end
end
local backpack = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if backpack then
for _, tool in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
if tool:IsA("Tool") then
local toolName = tool.Name:lower()
if toolName:find("bat", 1, true) or toolName:find("slap", 1, true) then
return tool
end
end
end
end
end
return nil
end
do
pcall(function()
local oldSphere = workspace:FindFirstChild("PredictionSphere")
if oldSphere then oldSphere:Destroy() end
end)
local Z = {
targetPlayer = nil,
lastTargetPos = nil,
targetVelocity = Vector3.zero,
smoothedVelocity = Vector3.zero,
velocityHistory = {},
accelerationHistory = {},
aerialVelocityHistory = {},
verticalVelocityHistory = {},
previousDirection = nil,
lastDirectionChangeTime = 0,
airborneTime = 0,
lastYVelocity = 0,
lastJumpTime = 0,
lastActivationTime = 0,
currentPing = 0.1,
realPingMs = 0,
}
local ZCFG = {
FOLLOW_SPEED = 55,
ACTIVATE_DISTANCE = 13,
MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE = 1,
PREDICTION_TIME = 0.22,
PREDICT_AHEAD = 3,
MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE = 150,
VELOCITY_SMOOTHING = 0.2,
MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY = 80,
SERVER_TICKRATE = 1 / 60,
MIN_PING_COMPENSATION = 0.03,
MAX_PING_COMPENSATION = 0.25,
ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT = 0.3,
DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME = 0.12,
QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER = 1.5,
GRAVITY = 196.2,
AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR = 0.8,
AERIAL_VELOCITY_DECAY = 0.95,
MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME = 0.08,
FALLING_SPEED_THRESHOLD = -15,
}
local zurichConn = nil
local function averageVector(history)
if #history == 0 then return Vector3.zero end
local total = Vector3.zero
for _, value in ipairs(history) do total += value end
return total / #history
end
local function pushHistory(history, value, maximum)
table.insert(history, value)
if #history > maximum then table.remove(history, 1) end
end
local function resetZurichTarget()
Z.targetPlayer = nil
Z.lastTargetPos = nil
Z.targetVelocity = Vector3.zero
Z.smoothedVelocity = Vector3.zero
Z.velocityHistory = {}
Z.accelerationHistory = {}
Z.aerialVelocityHistory = {}
Z.verticalVelocityHistory = {}
Z.previousDirection = nil
Z.airborneTime = 0
Z.lastYVelocity = 0
end
local function nearestZurichTarget(root)
local nearest, nearestDistance = nil, math.huge
for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if candidate ~= Player and candidate.Character then
local targetRoot = candidate.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local targetHumanoid = candidate.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if targetRoot and targetHumanoid and targetHumanoid.Health > 0 then
local distance = (root.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude
if distance < nearestDistance then nearest, nearestDistance = candidate, distance end
end
end
end
return nearest
end
local function rotateZurichRoot(root, direction)
if direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
local axis = root.CFrame.LookVector:Cross(direction.Unit)
local angle = math.asin(math.clamp(axis.Magnitude, -1, 1))
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = axis.Magnitude > 0.01 and axis.Unit * angle * 80 or Vector3.zero
end
local function sampleZurichPing()
local ok, value = pcall(function()
return game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
end)
if ok and type(value) == "number" then Z.realPingMs = math.floor(value) end
Z.currentPing = math.clamp(Z.realPingMs / 1000, ZCFG.MIN_PING_COMPENSATION, ZCFG.MAX_PING_COMPENSATION)
end
task.spawn(function()
while _G.__S11_STATE == s11RuntimeState do
pcall(sampleZurichPing)
task.wait(0.5)
end
end)
local function stopZurichAimbot()
if zurichConn then zurichConn:Disconnect(); zurichConn = nil end
local char = Player.Character
local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if humanoid then humanoid.AutoRotate = true end
if root then root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
resetZurichTarget()
Z.lastActivationTime = 0
end
local function startZurichAimbot(char)
char = char or Player.Character
local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not humanoid or not root then return end
stopZurichAimbot()
humanoid.AutoRotate = false
local bat = getBat()
if bat and bat.Parent ~= char then pcall(humanoid.EquipTool, humanoid, bat) end
zurichConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
if not toggleStates["Aimbot"] then stopZurichAimbot(); return end
local currentChar = Player.Character
local currentRoot = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local currentHumanoid = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not currentRoot or not currentHumanoid or currentHumanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
currentHumanoid.AutoRotate = false
root, humanoid = currentRoot, currentHumanoid
bat = currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or getBat()
if bat and bat.Parent ~= currentChar then pcall(currentHumanoid.EquipTool, currentHumanoid, bat) end
 
 
local markerCFrame = nil
if _G.__S11TPBatGetMarkerCFrame then
local ok, result = pcall(_G.__S11TPBatGetMarkerCFrame)
if ok then markerCFrame = result end
end
if markerCFrame then
local markedDirection = markerCFrame.Position - root.Position
if markedDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then rotateZurichRoot(root, markedDirection) end
if markedDirection.Magnitude > ZCFG.MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE then
local markedSpeed = tonumber(speedValues.CypherAimbotApproachSpeed) or ZCFG.FOLLOW_SPEED
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = markedDirection.Unit * math.clamp(markedSpeed, 1, 120)
else
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
end
return
end
Z.targetPlayer = nearestZurichTarget(root)
local targetChar = Z.targetPlayer and Z.targetPlayer.Character
local targetRoot = targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local targetHumanoid = targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not targetRoot or not targetHumanoid or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then resetZurichTarget(); return end
local targetPos = targetRoot.Position
local safeDt = math.max(dt, 1 / 240)
if Z.lastTargetPos then
local rawVelocity = (targetPos - Z.lastTargetPos) / safeDt
local velocityDelta = rawVelocity - Z.targetVelocity
if velocityDelta.Magnitude > ZCFG.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE then
rawVelocity = Z.targetVelocity + velocityDelta.Unit * ZCFG.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE
end
local horizontal = Vector3.new(rawVelocity.X, 0, rawVelocity.Z)
if horizontal.Magnitude > ZCFG.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY then
horizontal = horizontal.Unit * ZCFG.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY
rawVelocity = Vector3.new(horizontal.X, rawVelocity.Y, horizontal.Z)
end
pushHistory(Z.accelerationHistory, (rawVelocity - Z.targetVelocity) / safeDt, 4)
pushHistory(Z.velocityHistory, rawVelocity, 8)
pushHistory(Z.verticalVelocityHistory, rawVelocity.Y, 5)
Z.targetVelocity = rawVelocity
Z.smoothedVelocity = Z.smoothedVelocity:Lerp(rawVelocity, ZCFG.VELOCITY_SMOOTHING)
end
Z.lastTargetPos = targetPos
local airborne = targetHumanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
Z.airborneTime = airborne and (Z.airborneTime + safeDt) or 0
if airborne and Z.airborneTime >= ZCFG.MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME then
pushHistory(Z.aerialVelocityHistory, Z.targetVelocity, 6)
elseif not airborne then
Z.aerialVelocityHistory = {}
end
local predictionVelocity = Z.smoothedVelocity
if airborne and #Z.aerialVelocityHistory > 0 then
local aerial = averageVector(Z.aerialVelocityHistory)
predictionVelocity = Vector3.new(aerial.X, Z.targetVelocity.Y, aerial.Z) * ZCFG.AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR
end
local quickTurn = false
local horizontalVelocity = Vector3.new(Z.targetVelocity.X, 0, Z.targetVelocity.Z)
if horizontalVelocity.Magnitude > 5 then
local direction = horizontalVelocity.Unit
if Z.previousDirection and Z.previousDirection:Dot(direction) < 0.5 then
quickTurn = tick() - Z.lastDirectionChangeTime < ZCFG.DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME
Z.lastDirectionChangeTime = tick()
end
Z.previousDirection = direction
end
local serverDelay = Z.currentPing + ZCFG.SERVER_TICKRATE
if quickTurn then serverDelay *= ZCFG.QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER end
local predicted = targetPos + predictionVelocity * serverDelay
local acceleration = averageVector(Z.accelerationHistory)
predicted += acceleration * ZCFG.ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT * (serverDelay * serverDelay * 0.5)
local predictionTime = ZCFG.PREDICTION_TIME * 1.1
if airborne then
predicted += predictionVelocity * predictionTime
predicted += Vector3.new(0, -0.5 * ZCFG.GRAVITY * predictionTime * predictionTime, 0)
else
predicted += predictionVelocity * predictionTime
end
local flatPrediction = Vector3.new(predictionVelocity.X, 0, predictionVelocity.Z)
if flatPrediction.Magnitude > 1 then predicted += flatPrediction.Unit * ZCFG.PREDICT_AHEAD end
local toTarget = predicted - root.Position
rotateZurichRoot(root, toTarget)
if (targetPos - root.Position).Magnitude <= ZCFG.ACTIVATE_DISTANCE and tick() - Z.lastActivationTime >= 0.3 then
if bat then pcall(bat.Activate, bat) end
Z.lastActivationTime = tick()
end
if toTarget.Magnitude > ZCFG.MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE then
local speed = math.clamp(tonumber(speedValues.CypherAimbotApproachSpeed) or ZCFG.FOLLOW_SPEED, 1, 120)
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = toTarget.Unit * speed
else
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y * 0.5, 0)
end
end)
end
s11ImportedV1Start,s11ImportedV1Stop=startZurichAimbot,stopZurichAimbot
end
end do local s11Aim={connection=nil,attachmentName="S11AimbotMoveAttachment",moverName="S11AimbotMoveVelocity",minForce=1000000,maxForce=50000000,prediction=setmetatable({},{__mode="k"}),}local
function s11AimbotSpeed()local configured=tonumber(speedValues.S11AimbotApproachSpeed)or 55 if selectedMode=="Lagger"then configured=math.min(configured,tonumber(speedValues.LaggerBoost)or configured)end return math.clamp(configured,1,500)end
local function s11GetBatV3()local char=Player.Character if not char then return nil end local equipped=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")if
equipped and equipped.Name:lower():find("bat")then return equipped end local bp=Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")if bp then local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")for _,t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren())do if t:IsA("Tool")and t.Name:lower():find("bat")then
if hum then pcall(function()hum:EquipTool(t)end)end return char:FindFirstChild(t.Name)or t end end end return nil end local function s11NearestTarget(root)local nearest,nearestDistance=nil,math.huge for _,candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do if candidate~=Player and candidate.Character then local targetRoot=candidate.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")local
targetHumanoid=candidate.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")if targetRoot and targetHumanoid and targetHumanoid.Health>0 then local distance=(targetRoot.Position-root.Position).Magnitude if distance<nearestDistance then nearestDistance,nearest=distance,targetRoot
end end end end return nearest end local function s11ClearMover(root)root=root or(Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))if not root then return end local mover=root:FindFirstChild(s11Aim.moverName)local
attachment=root:FindFirstChild(s11Aim.attachmentName)if mover and mover:IsA("LinearVelocity")then mover:Destroy()end if attachment and attachment:IsA("Attachment")then attachment:Destroy()end end local function s11EnsureMover(root,speed)local
attachment=root:FindFirstChild(s11Aim.attachmentName)if attachment and not attachment:IsA("Attachment")then attachment:Destroy();attachment=nil end if not attachment then attachment=Instance.new("Attachment")attachment.Name=s11Aim.attachmentName
attachment.Parent=root end local mover=root:FindFirstChild(s11Aim.moverName)if not mover or not mover:IsA("LinearVelocity")then if mover then mover:Destroy()end mover=Instance.new("LinearVelocity")mover.Name=s11Aim.moverName mover.Attachment0=attachment
mover.RelativeTo=Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World mover.VelocityConstraintMode=Enum.VelocityConstraintMode.Vector mover.ForceLimitsEnabled=true mover.ForceLimitMode=Enum.ForceLimitMode.PerAxis mover.VectorVelocity=Vector3.zero mover.Parent=root
else mover.Attachment0=attachment end local force=math.clamp(root.AssemblyMass*math.max(speed*220,6000),s11Aim.minForce,s11Aim.maxForce)mover.MaxAxesForce=Vector3.new(force,force,force)mover.Enabled=true return mover end local function
s11ApplyMove(root,velocity,speed,dt)local mover=s11EnsureMover(root,speed)local alpha=1-math.exp(-math.max(dt or 1/60,0)*26)mover.VectorVelocity=mover.VectorVelocity:Lerp(velocity,alpha)end local function s11ResetMotion()local char=Player.Character
local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")local humanoid=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")if root then s11ClearMover(root)root.Anchored=false root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
local look=root.CFrame.LookVector local flat=Vector3.new(look.X,0,look.Z)flat=flat.Magnitude>0.01 and flat.Unit or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)root.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(root.Position,root.Position+flat)end if humanoid then humanoid.AutoRotate=true
humanoid.PlatformStand=false pcall(function()humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero,false)end)pcall(function()humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)end)end end local function s11Update(dt)if not toggleStates["Aimbot"]then return end
local char=Player.Character local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")local humanoid=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")if not char or not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 then return end local
bat=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")or s11GetBatV3()if bat and bat.Parent~=char then pcall(humanoid.EquipTool,humanoid,bat)end local target=nil local markerCFrame=nil if _G.__S11TPBatGetMarkerCFrame then local ok,result=pcall(_G.__S11TPBatGetMarkerCFrame)if
ok then markerCFrame=result end end if markerCFrame then target={Position=markerCFrame.Position}else target=s11NearestTarget(root)end if not target then humanoid.AutoRotate=true root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero s11ClearMover(root)return
end local speed=s11AimbotSpeed()humanoid.AutoRotate=false local now=tick()local targetPosition=target.Position local predictedVelocity=Vector3.zero if typeof(target)=="Instance"then local sample=s11Aim.prediction[target]if sample then
local elapsed=math.clamp(now-sample.time,1/120,0.25)local measured=(targetPosition-sample.pos)/elapsed sample.vel=sample.vel:Lerp(measured.Magnitude>80 and measured.Unit*80 or measured,0.25)sample.pos=targetPosition sample.time=now
predictedVelocity=sample.vel else s11Aim.prediction[target]={pos=targetPosition,time=now,vel=Vector3.zero}end end local predicted=targetPosition+predictedVelocity*math.clamp(predictedVelocity.Magnitude/130,0.05,0.12)+Vector3.new(0,1,0)local
delta=predicted-root.Position local flatDelta=Vector3.new(delta.X,0,delta.Z)if delta.Magnitude>0.01 and flatDelta.Magnitude>0.01 then local yawError=(math.deg(math.atan2(-flatDelta.X,-flatDelta.Z))-root.Orientation.Y+180)%360-180 local
pitchError=(math.deg(math.atan2(delta.Y,flatDelta.Magnitude))-root.Orientation.X+180)%360-180 local yaw=math.clamp(math.rad(yawError)*285,-28,28)local pitch=math.clamp(math.rad(pitchError)*285,-28,28)local facingYaw=math.rad(root.Orientation.Y)local
facing=Vector3.new(math.cos(facingYaw),0,-math.sin(facingYaw))root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.new(0,yaw,0)+facing*pitch else root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero end local lead=flatDelta.Magnitude>0.01 and flatDelta.Unit*2.8
or Vector3.zero local chaseDelta=predicted+lead+Vector3.new(0,4.75,0)-root.Position local chaseFlat=Vector3.new(chaseDelta.X,0,chaseDelta.Z)local horizontal=chaseFlat.Magnitude>0.1 and chaseFlat.Unit*speed or Vector3.zero local vertical=math.abs(chaseDelta.Y)>0.1
and Vector3.new(0,math.sign(chaseDelta.Y)*52,0)or Vector3.new(0,-2,0)s11ApplyMove(root,horizontal+vertical,speed,dt)if chaseFlat.Magnitude>0.5 then humanoid:Move(chaseFlat.Unit,false)end if bat and bat.Parent==char then pcall(function()bat:Activate()end)end
end local s11V1Engine={start=function()if s11ImportedV1Start then s11ImportedV1Start(Player.Character)end end,stop=function()if s11ImportedV1Stop then s11ImportedV1Stop()end end,setSpeed=function(value)local parsed=tonumber(value)if
not parsed or parsed~=parsed or parsed<=0 or parsed==math.huge then return math.clamp(tonumber(speedValues.S11AimbotApproachSpeed)or 55,1,120)end return math.clamp(math.floor(parsed+0.5),1,120)end,}_G.__S11V1SetSpeed=function(value)local
speed=tonumber(value)if not speed or speed~=speed or speed<=0 or speed==math.huge then return speedValues.S11AimbotApproachSpeed end local maximum=batAimbotMode=="V1"and 120 or 500 speed=math.clamp(math.floor(speed+0.5),1,maximum)if
batAimbotMode=="V1"then speed=s11V1Engine.setSpeed(speed)end speedValues.S11AimbotApproachSpeed=speed return speed end _G.__S11V1SetSpeed(speedValues.S11AimbotApproachSpeed)local function stopV1BatAimbot()s11V1Engine.stop()end local
function startV1BatAimbot()s11V1Engine.start()end local s11VisionV2Engine=(function()local player=Player local VERTICAL_SPEED=52 local DIST_OFFSET=-2.8 local HEIGHT_OFFSET=4.75 local VERTICAL_AIM_OFFSET=1 local TURN_SPEED=285 local
MAX_TURN_RATE=28 local MAX_PREDICTION_SPEED=80 local MAX_LEAD=0.12 local VELOCITY_SMOOTHING=0.25 local active=false local autoSwing=true local mirrorTP=true local renderConnection=nil local mirrorConnection=nil local predictions={}local
mirrorTracking={}local hadTarget=false local originalAutoRotate=nil local function findBatTool()local character=player.Character if not character then return nil end local backpack=player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")for _,container
in ipairs({character,backpack})do if container then for _,child in ipairs(container:GetChildren())do if child:IsA("Tool")and string.find(string.lower(child.Name),"bat",1,true)then return child end end end end return nil end local function
ensureBatEquipped(character,humanoid)local currentTool=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")if currentTool then return currentTool end local bat=findBatTool()if bat then pcall(function()humanoid:EquipTool(bat)end)end return bat end
local function nearestOpponent(myRoot)local nearest,nearestDistance=nil,math.huge for _,other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do if other~=player and other.Character then local root=other.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")local
humanoid=other.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")if root and humanoid and humanoid.Health>0 then local distance=(root.Position-myRoot.Position).Magnitude if distance<nearestDistance then nearest,nearestDistance=root,distance
end end end end return nearest end local function predictedVelocity(targetRoot)local now=tick()local stored=predictions[targetRoot]if not stored then predictions[targetRoot]={pos=targetRoot.Position,time=now,vel=Vector3.zero,}return
Vector3.zero end local dt=math.clamp(now-stored.time,1/120,0.25)local velocity=(targetRoot.Position-stored.pos)/dt if velocity.Magnitude>MAX_PREDICTION_SPEED then velocity=velocity.Unit*MAX_PREDICTION_SPEED end stored.vel=stored.vel:Lerp(velocity,VELOCITY_SMOOTHING)stored.pos=targetRoot.Position
stored.time=now return stored.vel end local function holdingBrainrot()local character=player.Character if not character then return false end if player:GetAttribute("Stealing")==true or player:GetAttribute("AntiKick")==true or character:GetAttribute("Stealing")==true
then return true end for _,name in ipairs({"Carrying","IsCarrying","Carried","Holding","Grabbed","HasGrab"})do local obj=character:FindFirstChild(name,true)if obj then if obj:IsA("BoolValue")and obj.Value then return true end if obj:IsA("ObjectValue")and
obj.Value then return true end if obj:IsA("StringValue")and obj.Value~=""then return true end end end for _,child in ipairs(character:GetChildren())do local name=child.Name:lower()if child:IsA("Tool")and not name:find("bat")and not
name:find("slap")and not name:find("medusa")and not name:find("head")and not name:find("stone")then return true end if child:IsA("Model")and child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)and(name:find("brainrot")or name:find("animal")or
name:find("carry")or name:find("grab")or name:find("steal")or name:find("hold"))then return true end end return false end local function countdownBlocking()local sound=workspace:FindFirstChild("Countdown")return sound and sound:IsA("Sound")and
sound.Playing and sound.TimePosition<5 end local function restoreMovement()local character=player.Character local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")if
humanoid then humanoid.AutoRotate=originalAutoRotate==nil and true or originalAutoRotate humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero,false)end if root then root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero end originalAutoRotate=nil hadTarget=false end local
function mirrorStep()if not active or not mirrorTP then table.clear(mirrorTracking)return end local character=player.Character local myRoot=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")if not myRoot then return end for
_,other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do if other~=player and other.Character then local root=other.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")local humanoid=other.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")if root and humanoid and
humanoid.Health>0 then local dx,dz=root.Position.X-myRoot.Position.X,root.Position.Z-myRoot.Position.Z if dx*dx+dz*dz<=64 then local previous=mirrorTracking[root]local y=root.Position.Y if previous and previous.prevY-y>=3 then local
_,yaw=myRoot.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()myRoot.CFrame=CFrame.new(myRoot.Position.X,-7,myRoot.Position.Z)*CFrame.Angles(0,yaw,0)myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero end mirrorTracking[root]={prevY=y}else mirrorTracking[root]=nil
end end end end end local setAimbot local function aimStep()if not active then return end if countdownBlocking()or holdingBrainrot()then setAimbot(false)task.defer(function()if batAimbotMode=="V2"and toggleStates["Aimbot"]then toggleStates["Aimbot"]=false
if FeaturePostToggle["Aimbot"]then FeaturePostToggle["Aimbot"](false)end if toggleVisualUpdaters["Aimbot"]then toggleVisualUpdaters["Aimbot"]()end end end)return end local character=player.Character local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")local
root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")if not character or not humanoid or not root or humanoid.Health<=0 then return end ensureBatEquipped(character,humanoid)local target=nearestOpponent(root)if not target then
humanoid.AutoRotate=originalAutoRotate==nil and true or originalAutoRotate root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero if hadTarget then humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero,false)end hadTarget=false return end hadTarget=true if originalAutoRotate==nil
then originalAutoRotate=humanoid.AutoRotate end humanoid.AutoRotate=false local velocity=predictedVelocity(target)local lead=math.clamp(velocity.Magnitude/130,0.05,MAX_LEAD)local aimPosition=target.Position+velocity*lead+Vector3.new(0,VERTICAL_AIM_OFFSET,0)local
displacement=aimPosition-root.Position local horizontal=Vector3.new(displacement.X,0,displacement.Z)if displacement.Magnitude>0.01 and horizontal.Magnitude>0.01 then local currentYaw=root.Orientation.Y local yawError=(math.deg(math.atan2(-horizontal.X,-horizontal.Z))-currentYaw+180)%360-180
local pitchError=(math.deg(math.atan2(displacement.Y,horizontal.Magnitude))-root.Orientation.X+180)%360-180 local yawAngular=math.clamp(math.rad(yawError)*TURN_SPEED,-MAX_TURN_RATE,MAX_TURN_RATE)local pitchAngular=math.clamp(math.rad(pitchError)*TURN_SPEED,-MAX_TURN_RATE,MAX_TURN_RATE)local
yaw=math.rad(root.Orientation.Y)local pitchAxis=Vector3.new(math.cos(yaw),0,-math.sin(yaw))root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.new(0,yawAngular,0)+pitchAxis*pitchAngular else root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero end local direction=displacement.Magnitude>0.01
and displacement.Unit or Vector3.zero local delta=aimPosition-direction*DIST_OFFSET+Vector3.new(0,HEIGHT_OFFSET,0)-root.Position local flat=Vector3.new(delta.X,0,delta.Z)local horizontalVelocity=flat.Magnitude>0.1 and flat.Unit*(58*math.clamp(tonumber(speedValues.S11AimbotApproachSpeed)or
55,1,500)/55)or Vector3.zero local verticalVelocity=math.abs(delta.Y)>0.1 and Vector3.new(0,math.sign(delta.Y)*VERTICAL_SPEED,0)or Vector3.new(0,-2,0)root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=horizontalVelocity+verticalVelocity if flat.Magnitude>0.5
then humanoid:Move(flat.Unit,false)end if autoSwing then local equipped=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")if equipped then pcall(function()equipped:Activate()end)end end end local refreshGUI setAimbot=function(value)value=value==true
if value and(holdingBrainrot()or countdownBlocking())then value=false end if value==active then if refreshGUI then refreshGUI()end return end active=value if active then table.clear(predictions)table.clear(mirrorTracking)hadTarget=false
renderConnection=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(aimStep)mirrorConnection=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(mirrorStep)else if renderConnection then renderConnection:Disconnect();renderConnection=nil end if mirrorConnection then mirrorConnection:Disconnect();mirrorConnection=nil
end table.clear(predictions)table.clear(mirrorTracking)restoreMovement()end if refreshGUI then refreshGUI()end end _G.__S11TrackConn(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()table.clear(predictions)table.clear(mirrorTracking)originalAutoRotate=nil
hadTarget=false end))return{start=function()setAimbot(true);return active end,stop=function()setAimbot(false)end,options=function(swingEnabled,mirrorEnabled)autoSwing=swingEnabled==true mirrorTP=mirrorEnabled==true end,}end)()local
function startS11BatAimbot()if _aimbotStopFollowing then pcall(_aimbotStopFollowing)end if s11Aim.connection then return end local char=Player.Character local humanoid=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")local bat=s11GetBatV3()if
char and humanoid and bat and bat.Parent~=char then pcall(humanoid.EquipTool,humanoid,bat)end s11Aim.prediction=setmetatable({},{__mode="k"})s11ClearMover()s11Aim.connection=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(s11Update)end local function
stopS11BatAimbot()if not s11Aim.connection then return end s11Aim.connection:Disconnect()s11Aim.connection=nil s11ResetMotion()end 
-- S11 V4 / motor independiente: persecucion directa tipo V1 + movimiento intermitente.
local S11V4 = (function()
    local R = game:GetService("RunService")
    local P = game:GetService("Players")
    local S = game:GetService("Stats")
    local connection, active, lastToolScan = nil, false, 0
    local lockedRoot, observedPos, observedAt, lastMotionAt = nil, nil, 0, 0
    local filteredVelocity, fastUntil, jitterUntil, reverseUntil = Vector3.zero, 0, 0, 0
    local lastAttack, totalAttacks, lastStatus, pingSeconds = 0, 0, 0, 0.08
    local lastPingRead, previousFacingHumanoid = 0, nil
    local originalAutoRotate = nil
    local lastError = nil
    local nowErrorLog = nil
    local lastTool = nil

    local function flat(v)
        return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
    end

    local function publish(state, other)
        _G.__S11AimbotV4Status = {
            enabled = active,
            phase = state,
            target = other and other.Name or "",
            speed = math.floor(filteredVelocity.Magnitude + 0.5),
            detectedFast = tick() < fastUntil,
            detectedJitter = tick() < jitterUntil,
            pingMs = math.floor(pingSeconds * 1000 + 0.5),
            swingAttempts = totalAttacks,
            lastError = lastError
        }
    end

    local function resetTrack(targetRoot, now)
        lockedRoot = targetRoot
        observedPos = targetRoot and targetRoot.Position or nil
        observedAt = now
        lastMotionAt = now
        filteredVelocity = Vector3.zero
        fastUntil, jitterUntil, reverseUntil = 0, 0, 0
    end

    local function validRoot(part)
        if not part or not part.Parent then return false end
        local human = part.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        return human ~= nil and human.Health > 0
    end

    local function selectTarget(root, now)
        local nearest, nearestDistance = nil, math.huge
        for _, plr in ipairs(P:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= Player then
                local ch = plr.Character
                local enemyRoot = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if validRoot(enemyRoot) then
                    local distance = (enemyRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                    if distance < nearestDistance then
                        nearest, nearestDistance = enemyRoot, distance
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if validRoot(lockedRoot) then
            local currentDist = (lockedRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
            if currentDist <= nearestDistance * 1.35 + 5 then
                nearest = lockedRoot
            end
        end
        if nearest ~= lockedRoot then resetTrack(nearest, now) end
        return nearest
    end

    local function trackMotion(part, now)
        local pos = part.Position
        if not observedPos then resetTrack(part, now); return end
        local difference = flat(pos - observedPos)
        local travelled = difference.Magnitude
        local elapsed = math.max(now - observedAt, 1 / 240)
        if travelled > 0.08 then
            local motionDt = math.max(now - lastMotionAt, 1 / 120)
            local speed = travelled / motionDt
            local isJump = travelled > 24 or (travelled > 6 and travelled / elapsed > 440)
            if isJump then
                jitterUntil = now + 0.9
                filteredVelocity *= 0.35
            else
                local measured = difference / motionDt
                if measured.Magnitude > 260 then measured = measured.Unit * 260 end
                if filteredVelocity.Magnitude > 10 and measured.Magnitude > 10 and
                    filteredVelocity:Dot(measured) < -0.4 * filteredVelocity.Magnitude * measured.Magnitude then
                    reverseUntil = now + 0.18
                end
                filteredVelocity = filteredVelocity:Lerp(measured, speed >= 70 and 0.66 or 0.38)
                if speed >= 70 then fastUntil = now + 0.7 end
                if motionDt > 0.2 then jitterUntil = now + 0.8 end
            end
            lastMotionAt = now
        elseif now - lastMotionAt > 0.20 then
            -- Posicion congelada: no extrapolar indefinidamente un objetivo antiguo.
            if filteredVelocity.Magnitude > 8 then jitterUntil = now + 0.3 end
            filteredVelocity *= math.exp(-math.min(elapsed, 0.1) * 7)
        end
        observedPos = pos
        observedAt = now
    end

    local function findBat(character)
        local function isBat(obj)
            return obj:IsA("Tool") and (obj.Name:lower():find("bat", 1, true)
                or obj.Name:lower():find("slap", 1, true))
        end
        for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if isBat(obj) then return obj end
        end
        local bag = Player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if bag then
            for _, obj in ipairs(bag:GetChildren()) do
                if isBat(obj) then return obj end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function update(dt)
        if not active then return end
        if not toggleStates["Aimbot"] or batAimbotMode ~= "V4" then
            return
        end
        local char = Player.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local human = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not human or human.Health <= 0 then
            if tick() - lastStatus > 0.5 then publish("WAIT_CHARACTER") lastStatus = tick() end
            return
        end
        local now = tick()
        if previousFacingHumanoid ~= human then
            if previousFacingHumanoid and previousFacingHumanoid.Parent and originalAutoRotate ~= nil then
                previousFacingHumanoid.AutoRotate = originalAutoRotate
            end
            previousFacingHumanoid = human
            originalAutoRotate = human.AutoRotate
            lastToolScan = 0
            resetTrack(nil, now)
        end
        human.AutoRotate = false
        if now - lastPingRead > 0.8 then
            lastPingRead = now
            local ok, ms = pcall(function()
                return S.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
            end)
            if ok and type(ms) == "number" then
                pingSeconds = math.clamp(ms / 1000, 0.02, 0.28)
            end
        end
        if now - lastToolScan >= 0.25 or not lastTool or not lastTool.Parent then
            lastToolScan = now
            lastTool = findBat(char)
            if lastTool and lastTool.Parent ~= char then
                pcall(function() human:EquipTool(lastTool) end)
            end
        end

        local marked = nil
        if type(_G.__S11TPBatGetMarkerCFrame) == "function" then
            local ok, cf = pcall(_G.__S11TPBatGetMarkerCFrame)
            if ok and typeof(cf) == "CFrame" then marked = cf.Position end
        end
        local target = nil
        local name = ""
        if marked then
            if lockedRoot then resetTrack(nil, now) end
        else
            target = selectTarget(root, now)
        end
        if not marked and not target then
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            if now - lastStatus > 0.3 then publish("NO_TARGET") lastStatus = now end
            return
        end

        local targetPos = marked or target.Position
        if target then trackMotion(target, now) end
        if target and target.Parent then
            local targetPlayer = P:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target.Parent)
            name = targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Name or target.Parent.Name
        end
        local fast = now < fastUntil
        local jitter = now < jitterUntil
        local v = filteredVelocity
        local lead = math.clamp(0.07 + pingSeconds * 0.75 + v.Magnitude / 1800, 0.05, 0.24)
        if now < reverseUntil then lead *= 0.35 end
        if jitter then lead = math.min(lead, 0.045) end
        local offset = v * lead
        local maxLead = jitter and 3 or (fast and 17 or 9)
        if offset.Magnitude > maxLead then offset = offset.Unit * maxLead end
        local predicted = targetPos + offset
        local horizontal = flat(predicted - root.Position)
        local actual = flat(targetPos - root.Position)
        local realDist = (targetPos - root.Position).Magnitude

        -- V1-type direct chase. Body rotates only in yaw: no flips from aiming upward.
        if horizontal.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            local facing = flat(root.CFrame.LookVector)
            if facing.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                local a, b = facing.Unit, horizontal.Unit
                local turnAngle = math.atan2(a.Z * b.X - a.X * b.Z, a:Dot(b))
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, math.clamp(turnAngle * 34, -24, 24), 0)
            end
        end
        local baseSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(speedValues.S11AimbotApproachSpeed) or 55, 15, 160)
        local chaseSpeed = baseSpeed
        if fast then chaseSpeed = math.clamp(math.max(baseSpeed, v.Magnitude * 1.25 + 45), 40, 205)
        elseif jitter then chaseSpeed = math.max(baseSpeed, 86) end
        local move = Vector3.zero
        if horizontal.Magnitude > 2.4 then
            move = horizontal.Unit * chaseSpeed
        elseif horizontal.Magnitude > 0.4 then
            move = horizontal.Unit * math.min(chaseSpeed, 18)
        end
        -- Preserve gravity on flat terrain; lift only when enemy is above.
        local ydiff = targetPos.Y - root.Position.Y
        local yspeed = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
        if ydiff > 2.8 then yspeed = math.clamp(ydiff * 4, 0, 35)
        elseif ydiff < -6 then yspeed = math.clamp(ydiff * 3, -28, 0) end
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(move.X, yspeed, move.Z)

        -- Swing on real contact AND just before a fast opponent crosses the reach.
        local reach = 12.5
        local batReady = lastTool and lastTool.Parent == char
        local relativeVel = v - flat(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity)
        local soon = flat(targetPos - root.Position) + relativeVel * math.clamp(pingSeconds * 0.8 + 0.08, 0.07, 0.21)
        local aboutToConnect = fast and actual.Magnitude <= 18 and soon.Magnitude <= reach
        local inRange = realDist <= (jitter and 14 or reach) and math.abs(ydiff) <= 7.5
        local interval = (fast or jitter) and 0.15 or 0.24
        if target and batReady and lastTool.Enabled and (inRange or aboutToConnect) and now - lastAttack >= interval then
            local ok, err = pcall(function() lastTool:Activate() end)
            if ok then
                lastAttack = now
                totalAttacks += 1
            else
                lastError = tostring(err)
            end
        end
        if now - lastStatus >= 0.2 then
            lastStatus = now
            publish(marked and "MARKER" or jitter and "JITTER" or fast and "SPEED" or "FOLLOWING", name and {Name=name} or nil)
            local status = _G.__S11AimbotV4Status
            if status then
                status.chaseSpeed = math.floor(chaseSpeed + 0.5)
                status.attackInterval = interval
                status.distance = math.floor(realDist * 10 + 0.5) / 10
                status.batReady = not not batReady
            end
        end
    end

    local Engine = {}
    function Engine.start()
        if connection then return true end
        active = true
        lastError = nil
        nowErrorLog = nil
        lastToolScan = 0
        lastAttack = 0
        totalAttacks = 0
        resetTrack(nil, tick())
        publish("STARTING")
        connection = R.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not active then return end
            if not toggleStates["Aimbot"] or batAimbotMode ~= "V4" then
                Engine.stop()
                return
            end
            local ok, err = pcall(update, dt)
            if not ok then
                lastError = tostring(err)
                publish("ERROR")
                if nowErrorLog == nil or tick() - nowErrorLog > 1 then
                    nowErrorLog = tick()
                    warn("[S11 AIMBOT V4] " .. lastError)
                end
            end
        end)
        return true
    end

    function Engine.stop(clearStatus)
        active = false
        if connection then connection:Disconnect(); connection = nil end
        if previousFacingHumanoid and previousFacingHumanoid.Parent then
            previousFacingHumanoid.AutoRotate = originalAutoRotate == nil and true or originalAutoRotate
        end
        local ch = Player.Character
        local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
        previousFacingHumanoid = nil
        originalAutoRotate = nil
        lastTool = nil
        resetTrack(nil, tick())
        if clearStatus ~= false then _G.__S11AimbotV4Status = nil end
    end

    function Engine.getStatus()
        return _G.__S11AimbotV4Status
    end
    return Engine
end)()
_G.__S11AimbotV4Engine = S11V4

local function stopSelectedAimbot()S11V4.stop();stopV1BatAimbot()s11VisionV2Engine.stop()stopS11BatAimbot()end local
function startSelectedAimbot()stopSelectedAimbot()if not toggleStates["Aimbot"]then return end if batAimbotMode=="V4"then S11V4.start()elseif batAimbotMode=="V3"then startS11BatAimbot()elseif batAimbotMode=="V2"then if not s11VisionV2Engine.start()then task.defer(function()if
batAimbotMode=="V2"and toggleStates["Aimbot"]then toggleStates["Aimbot"]=false if FeaturePostToggle["Aimbot"]then FeaturePostToggle["Aimbot"](false)end if toggleVisualUpdaters["Aimbot"]then toggleVisualUpdaters["Aimbot"]()end end end)end
else startV1BatAimbot()end end _G.__S11SetBatAimbotMode=function(mode)local selected=(mode=="V2"or mode=="V3"or mode=="V4")and mode or"V1"if selected~=batAimbotMode then batAimbotMode=selected if _G.__S11V1SetSpeed then _G.__S11V1SetSpeed(speedValues.S11AimbotApproachSpeed)end
if _G.__S11RefreshAimbotSpeedUI then pcall(_G.__S11RefreshAimbotSpeedUI)end if toggleStates["Aimbot"]then startSelectedAimbot()end saveConfig()end for _,btn in ipairs(_G.__S11AimbotVersionButtons or{})do if btn and btn.Parent then btn.Text=batAimbotMode
end end return batAimbotMode end FeaturePostToggle["Aimbot"]=function(active)if active then if _G.__stopSpeedBoost then _G.__stopSpeedBoost()end if autoplayActive or toggleStates["Autoplay"]then _G.__stopAutoplay()end deactivateOtherAimbots("Aimbot")if

    if not player then return false end
    if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return true end
    local char = player.Character
    return char ~= nil and char:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
end
local function syncAutoOnSteal(animated)
    local realSteal = getCurrentStealingState()
    isStealing = realSteal
    if autoState and realSteal ~= bypassRunning then
        setBypassState(realSteal, animated)
    end

local function clampAntiBatPosition(x,y)
local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
local viewport=camera and camera.ViewportSize
if not viewport or viewport.X<=0 or viewport.Y<=0 then return x,y end
local scale=isMobile and 0.86 or 1
local w=window and window.AbsoluteSize.X or 0
local h=window and window.AbsoluteSize.Y or 0
if w<10 then w=panelWidth*scale end
if h<10 then h=(minimized and 44 or panelHeight)*scale end
local margin=6
return math.clamp(x,margin,math.max(margin,viewport.X-w-margin)),
math.clamp(y,margin,math.max(margin,viewport.Y-h-margin))
end
local function keepAntiBatVisible()
 
if savedGuiState.dragging or not window or not window.Parent then return end
local current=window.AbsolutePosition
local x,y=clampAntiBatPosition(current.X,current.Y)
window.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(x+0.5),math.floor(y+0.5))
savedGuiState.position=window.Position
end

local function setMinimized(value)
if not window or not window.Parent then return end
local fixedPosition=window.Position
minimized=value==true
savedGuiState.minimized=minimized
collapseButton.Text=minimized and "+" or "−"
local content=window:FindFirstChild("AntiBatContent")
if content then content.Visible=not minimized end
if collapsedState then collapsedState.Visible=minimized end
if pageLabel then pageLabel.Visible=not minimized end
for _,arrow in ipairs(arrows)do arrow.Visible=not minimized end
window.Size=minimized and UDim2.fromOffset(panelWidth,44) or UDim2.fromOffset(panelWidth,panelHeight)
window.Position=fixedPosition
savedGuiState.position=fixedPosition
end

local dragging,dragInput,dragStart,frameStart=false,nil,nil,nil
local function dragZone(name,x,y,w,h)
local handle=Instance.new("Frame")
handle.Name=name
handle.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w,h)
handle.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(x,y)
handle.BackgroundTransparency=1
handle.BorderSizePixel=0
handle.Active=true
handle.ZIndex=20  
handle.Parent=window
connect(handle.InputBegan,function(input)
if dragging then return end
local t=input.UserInputType
if t~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and t~=Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
dragging=true
savedGuiState.dragging=true
 
savedGuiState.userMoved=true
dragInput=input
dragStart=input.Position
 
frameStart=window.Position
end)
end
 
dragZone("DragBar",0,0,158,38)
dragZone("DragTitle",17,40,252,19)
dragZone("DragLeftEdge",0,43,16,102)
dragZone("DragRightEdge",270,43,16,102)
dragZone("DragBottom",0,146,286,40)
connect(UserInputService.InputChanged,function(input)
if not dragging or not dragStart or not dragInput or not window or not window.Parent then return end
local t=dragInput.UserInputType
if t==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
if input~=dragInput then return end
elseif input.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
return
end
local diff=input.Position-dragStart
if diff.Magnitude<2 then return end
 
local viewport=workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
if not viewport or viewport.X<=0 or viewport.Y<=0 then return end
local originX=frameStart.X.Scale*viewport.X+frameStart.X.Offset
local originY=frameStart.Y.Scale*viewport.Y+frameStart.Y.Offset
local x,y=clampAntiBatPosition(originX+diff.X,originY+diff.Y)
window.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(x+0.5),math.floor(y+0.5))
savedGuiState.position=window.Position
end)
connect(UserInputService.InputEnded,function(input)
if not dragging or not dragInput then return end
 
if (dragInput.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
or (dragInput.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input==dragInput) then
dragging=false
savedGuiState.dragging=false
dragInput,dragStart,frameStart=nil,nil,nil
if window and window.Parent then savedGuiState.position=window.Position end
end
end)applyBackground(bgIndex,false)if savedGuiState.minimized==true then setMinimized(true)end task.defer(function() RunService.RenderStepped:Wait() keepAntiBatVisible() end)refresh()end _G.__positionAntiBatNearBypass=positionBesideBypass

-- ========== SUNBURN SONGS (from Sunburn.lua) ==========
SunUI = SunUI or {}
SunUI.palette = SunUI.palette or {
    ink = Color3.fromRGB(255, 248, 220),
    muted = Color3.fromRGB(200, 180, 120),
    gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0),
    coral = Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 40),
    base = Color3.fromRGB(18, 12, 2),
    panel = Color3.fromRGB(40, 28, 8),
    edge = Color3.fromRGB(140, 100, 20),
    secondary = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0),
}
SunUI.assetCache = SunUI.assetCache or {}
SunUI.assetErrors = SunUI.assetErrors or {}
SunUI.embedded = SunUI.embedded or {}
SunUI.remoteAssets = SunUI.remoteAssets or {}
SunUI.backgroundRevision = SunUI.backgroundRevision or 0
function SunUI.materialize(key)
    if SunUI.assetCache[key] then return SunUI.assetCache[key] end
    return nil
end
function SunUI.asset(index)
    return "rbxassetid://72681569130396"
end
function SunUI.scene(parent, z, options)
    options = options or {}
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = "SunUIScene"
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    frame.ZIndex = z or 1
    frame.Parent = parent
    local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    img.Name = "SceneImage"
    img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    img.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    img.Image = options.image or "rbxassetid://72681569130396"
    img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    img.ImageTransparency = 0.35
    img.ZIndex = (z or 1)
    img.Parent = frame
    if options.corner then
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, options.corner)
        c.Parent = frame
        local c2 = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c2.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, options.corner)
        c2.Parent = img
    end
    return {root = frame, image = img}
end
function SunUI.tintScene(scene, color)
    if scene and scene.image then
        pcall(function() scene.image.ImageColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120) end)
    end
end
function SunUI.trackColor(object) end
function SunUI.trackSurface(root) end
function SunUI.applySurface(root) end


-- Sunburn Songs: created on demand and reopened from Settings > Music.
SunUI.songsController = nil
SunUI.songsOpening = false
if type(getThemeColor) ~= "function" then
    function getThemeColor()
        return Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0)
    end
end

-- local helpers required by the songs UI
local function getThemeColor()
    return Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0)
end

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")

local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
local UIS = UserInputService
local HS = HttpService

pcall(function()
    local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pg then
        local old = pg:FindFirstChild("SunburnSongs")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
    end
end)
