local FearMusic = {
    sound = nil,
    index = 1,
    playing = false,
    paused = false,
    loading = false,
    looped = false,
    volume = 0.85,
    gui = nil,
    player = nil,
    library = nil,
    token = 0,
    playerScale = 1.00,
    libraryScale = 1.00,
    musicSizeLevel = 5,
    favorites = {},
    playlist = {},
    view = "all",
    firstPlayPending = true,
}

local FEAR_MUSIC_CONFIG = "SunburnSongs_Config.json"
local function _fearMusicSave()
    pcall(function()
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
        writefile(FEAR_MUSIC_CONFIG, HS:JSONEncode({
            looped = false,
            volume = FearMusic.volume,
            index = FearMusic.index,
            playerScale = FearMusic.playerScale,
            libraryScale = FearMusic.libraryScale,
            musicSizeLevel = FearMusic.musicSizeLevel,
            favorites = FearMusic.favorites,
            playlist = FearMusic.playlist,
            view = FearMusic.view,
        }))
    end)
end

local function _fearMusicLoad()
    pcall(function()
        if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(FEAR_MUSIC_CONFIG) then return end
        local data = HS:JSONDecode(readfile(FEAR_MUSIC_CONFIG))
        if type(data) ~= "table" then return end
        FearMusic.looped = false
        if tonumber(data.volume) then FearMusic.volume = math.clamp(tonumber(data.volume), 0, 1.5) end
        if tonumber(data.index) then FearMusic.index = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(data.index)), 1, #M4LWARE_JUKEBOX) end
        if tonumber(data.musicSizeLevel) then
            FearMusic.musicSizeLevel = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(data.musicSizeLevel) + 0.5), 1, 10)
            FearMusic.playerScale = 0.5 + (FearMusic.musicSizeLevel * 0.1)
            FearMusic.libraryScale = FearMusic.playerScale
        else
            -- Primera vez usando el selector 1-10: siempre empieza en 5.
            FearMusic.musicSizeLevel = 5
            FearMusic.playerScale = 1.0
            FearMusic.libraryScale = 1.0
        end
        if type(data.favorites) == "table" then FearMusic.favorites = data.favorites end
        if type(data.playlist) == "table" then FearMusic.playlist = data.playlist end
        if data.view == "all" or data.view == "liked" or data.view == "playlist" then FearMusic.view = data.view end
    end)
end
_fearMusicLoad()
FearMusic.looped = false

local function _fearFileExists(path)
    if type(isfile) ~= "function" then return false end
    local ok, result = pcall(isfile, path)
    return ok and result == true
end

local function _fearDownloadSong(option)
    if not option then return false end
    if option.embedded then return SunUI.materialize(option.embedded) ~= nil end
    if not option.url or type(writefile) ~= "function" then return false end
    if _fearFileExists(option.file) then return true end
    local data = nil
    local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
    if type(req) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            local result = req({Url=option.url, Method="GET", Headers={['User-Agent']='Mozilla/5.0'}})
            if result then data = result.Body or result.body end
        end)
    end
    if type(data) ~= "string" or #data < 1024 then
        pcall(function() data = game:HttpGet(option.url, true) end)
    end
    if type(data) ~= "string" or #data < 1024 then return false end
    local ok = pcall(writefile, option.file, data)
    return ok
end

local _fearCoverAssets = {}

local function _fearSongKey(option)
    if not option then return "" end
    return tostring(option.libraryKey or option.file or option.name or "")
end

local function _fearUrlEncode(value)
    value = tostring(value or "")
    value = value:gsub("\n", "\r\n")
    value = value:gsub("([^%w%-_%.~ ])", function(c)
        return string.format("%%%02X", string.byte(c))
    end)
    return value:gsub(" ", "%%20")
end

local function _fearHttpGet(url)
    local body = nil
    local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
    if type(req) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            local result = req({
                Url = url,
                Method = "GET",
                Headers = {["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0"}
            })
            if result then body = result.Body or result.body end
        end)
    end
    if type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then
        pcall(function() body = game:HttpGet(url, true) end)
    end
    return type(body) == "string" and body or nil
end

local function _fearGetCoverAsset(option, index)
    if not option then return nil end
    local key = _fearSongKey(option)
    if _fearCoverAssets[key] then return _fearCoverAssets[key] end

    local assetFn = getcustomasset or getsynasset
    if type(assetFn) ~= "function" or type(writefile) ~= "function" then return nil end

    local safeKey = tostring(option.file or option.name or index or "song")
    safeKey = safeKey:gsub("[^%w_%-]", "_")
    if #safeKey > 90 then safeKey = safeKey:sub(1, 90) end
    local coverFile = "FearHubExactCover_" .. safeKey .. ".jpg"
    if _fearFileExists(coverFile) then
        local ok, asset = pcall(assetFn, coverFile)
        if ok and type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "" then
            _fearCoverAssets[key] = asset
            return asset
        end
    end

    local coverUrl = option.coverUrl
    if type(coverUrl) ~= "string" or coverUrl == "" then return nil end

    local imageData = _fearHttpGet(coverUrl)
    if type(imageData) ~= "string" or #imageData < 512 then return nil end
    if not pcall(writefile, coverFile, imageData) then return nil end

    local ok, asset = pcall(assetFn, coverFile)
    if ok and type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "" then
        _fearCoverAssets[key] = asset
        return asset
    end
    return nil
end

local function _fearDestroySound()
    FearMusic.token = FearMusic.token + 1
    if FearMusic.sound then
        pcall(function() FearMusic.sound:Stop() end)
        pcall(function() FearMusic.sound:Destroy() end)
        FearMusic.sound = nil
    end
    FearMusic.playing = false
    FearMusic.paused = false
    FearMusic.loading = false
end

local function _fearCreateSound(index)
    local option = M4LWARE_JUKEBOX[index]
    if not option then return nil end
    local assetFn = getcustomasset or getsynasset
    if type(assetFn) ~= "function" then return nil end
    if option.embedded and not SunUI.materialize(option.embedded) then return nil end
    if not _fearFileExists(option.file) then
        if not _fearDownloadSong(option) then return nil end
    end
    local okAsset, asset = pcall(assetFn, option.file)
    if not okAsset or type(asset) ~= "string" or asset == "" then return nil end
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.Name = "SunburnSongsSound"
    sound.SoundId = asset
    sound.Volume = FearMusic.volume
    sound.Looped = false
    sound.Parent = SoundService
    if option.startAt then pcall(function() sound.TimePosition = option.startAt end) end
    return sound
end

local _fearRefreshMusicUI = function() end

local function _fearSongAllowedInView(index, view)
    local option = M4LWARE_JUKEBOX[index]
    if not option then return false end
    if view == "liked" then
        return FearMusic.favorites[_fearSongKey(option)] == true
    elseif view == "playlist" then
        return FearMusic.playlist[_fearSongKey(option)] == true
    end
    return true
end

local function _fearFirstSongInView(view)
    for i = 1, #M4LWARE_JUKEBOX do
        if _fearSongAllowedInView(i, view) then
            return i
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function _fearAdjacentSongInView(currentIndex, direction, view)
    direction = direction < 0 and -1 or 1
    if direction > 0 then
        for i = currentIndex + 1, #M4LWARE_JUKEBOX do
            if _fearSongAllowedInView(i, view) then
                return i
            end
        end
    else
        for i = currentIndex - 1, 1, -1 do
            if _fearSongAllowedInView(i, view) then
                return i
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function _fearRandomSongInView(view, avoidIndex)
    local choices = {}
    for i = 1, #M4LWARE_JUKEBOX do
        if _fearSongAllowedInView(i, view) and i ~= avoidIndex then
            table.insert(choices, i)
        end
    end

    -- Si solo existe una canción disponible, permite repetirla.
    if #choices == 0 and avoidIndex and _fearSongAllowedInView(avoidIndex, view) then
        return avoidIndex
    end
    if #choices == 0 then return nil end

    return choices[math.random(1, #choices)]
end

local function _fearPlaySong(index, reset)
    if #M4LWARE_JUKEBOX == 0 then return end
    if not index or index < 1 or index > #M4LWARE_JUKEBOX then return end
    FearMusic.firstPlayPending = false

    if FearMusic.sound and FearMusic.paused and FearMusic.index == index and not reset then
        local ok = pcall(function() FearMusic.sound:Resume() end)
        if not ok then pcall(function() FearMusic.sound:Play() end) end
        FearMusic.paused = false
        FearMusic.playing = true
        _fearRefreshMusicUI()
        return
    end

    FearMusic.index = index
    _fearDestroySound()
    FearMusic.loading = true
    FearMusic.index = index
    local myToken = FearMusic.token
    _fearRefreshMusicUI()

    task.spawn(function()
        local sound = _fearCreateSound(index)
        if myToken ~= FearMusic.token then
            if sound then pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end
            return
        end
        FearMusic.loading = false
        if not sound then
            _fearRefreshMusicUI()
            return
        end
        FearMusic.sound = sound
        sound.Looped = false
        sound.Volume = FearMusic.volume
        local option = M4LWARE_JUKEBOX[index]
        if option and option.startAt then pcall(function() sound.TimePosition = option.startAt end) end
        sound.Ended:Connect(function()
            if FearMusic.sound ~= sound then return end
            FearMusic.playing = false
            FearMusic.paused = false
            local nextIndex = _fearRandomSongInView(FearMusic.view, index)
            if nextIndex then
                task.defer(function() _fearPlaySong(nextIndex, true) end)
            else
                _fearRefreshMusicUI()
            end
        end)
        pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
        FearMusic.playing = true
        FearMusic.paused = false
        _fearMusicSave()
        _fearRefreshMusicUI()
    end)
end

local function _fearTogglePlay()
    if not FearMusic.sound then
        local index
        if FearMusic.firstPlayPending then
            index = _fearRandomSongInView(FearMusic.view, nil)
        else
            index = FearMusic.index
            if not _fearSongAllowedInView(index, FearMusic.view) then
                index = _fearFirstSongInView(FearMusic.view)
            end
        end
        if index then _fearPlaySong(index, true) end
        return
    end
    if not FearMusic.playing and not FearMusic.paused then
        _fearPlaySong(FearMusic.index or _fearFirstSongInView(FearMusic.view), true)
        return
    end
    if FearMusic.playing then
        pcall(function() FearMusic.sound:Pause() end)
        FearMusic.playing = false
        FearMusic.paused = true
    else
        local ok = pcall(function() FearMusic.sound:Resume() end)
        if not ok then pcall(function() FearMusic.sound:Play() end) end
        FearMusic.playing = true
        FearMusic.paused = false
    end
    _fearRefreshMusicUI()
end

local function _fearFormatTime(seconds)
    seconds = math.max(0, tonumber(seconds) or 0)
    local n = math.floor(seconds + 0.5)
    return string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(n/60), n%60)
end

    local function countMap(map)
        local count = 0
        for _, option in ipairs(M4LWARE_JUKEBOX) do
            if map[_fearSongKey(option)] == true then count = count + 1 end
        end
        return count
    end

    local refreshRows
    local refreshNav

    local function setCover(imageLabel, fallback, asset)
        if asset and asset ~= "" then
            imageLabel.Image = asset
            imageLabel.Visible = true
            fallback.Visible = false
        else
            imageLabel.Image = ""
            imageLabel.Visible = false
            fallback.Visible = true
        end
    end

    local function requestCover(index)
        if coverPending[index] then return end
        local option = M4LWARE_JUKEBOX[index]
        if not option then return end
        local key = _fearSongKey(option)
        local cached = _fearCoverAssets[key]
        if cached then
            local entry = rows[index]
            if entry then setCover(entry.cover, entry.fallback, cached) end
            if FearMusic.index == index then setCover(nowCover, nowFallback, cached) end
            return
        end
        coverPending[index] = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local asset = _fearGetCoverAsset(option, index)
            coverPending[index] = nil
            if not gui.Parent then return end
            if asset then
                local entry = rows[index]
                if entry then setCover(entry.cover, entry.fallback, asset) end
                if FearMusic.index == index then setCover(nowCover, nowFallback, asset) end
            end
        end)
    end

    for i, option in ipairs(M4LWARE_JUKEBOX) do
        local row = Instance.new("Frame", list)
        row.Name = "Song_" .. tostring(i)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1,-5,0,56)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = PANEL
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.ClipsDescendants = true
        row.LayoutOrder = i
        row.ZIndex = 105
        round(row, 12)
        local rowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", row)
        rowStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(62, 62, 70)
        rowStroke.Transparency = 0.72
        rowStroke.Thickness = 1

        local playArea = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        playArea.Size = UDim2.new(1,-78,1,0)
        playArea.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        playArea.BorderSizePixel = 0
        playArea.Text = ""
        playArea.AutoButtonColor = false
        playArea.ZIndex = 106

        local selectedBar = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        selectedBar.Name = "SelectedBar"
        selectedBar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 36)
        selectedBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -18)
        selectedBar.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE2
        selectedBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        selectedBar.Visible = false
        selectedBar.ZIndex = 111
        round(selectedBar, 3)

        local indexLabel = makeText(row, tostring(i), UDim2.fromOffset(24,56), UDim2.fromOffset(3,0), Enum.Font.Gotham, 8, MUTED2, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, 107)

        local coverBg = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        coverBg.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42,42)
        coverBg.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(31,7)
        coverBg.BackgroundColor3 = CARD
        coverBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
        coverBg.ClipsDescendants = true
        coverBg.ZIndex = 107
        round(coverBg, 9)
        local fallback = makeText(coverBg, "♫", UDim2.fromScale(1,1), UDim2.new(), Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 14, MUTED, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, 108)
        local cover = Instance.new("ImageLabel", coverBg)
        cover.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
        cover.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        cover.Image = ""
        cover.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        cover.Visible = false
        cover.ZIndex = 109

        local title = makeText(row, option.name or "Unknown", UDim2.new(1,-176,0,21), UDim2.fromOffset(82,7), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 10, WHITE2, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, 108)
        local artist = makeText(row, (option.preview and "PREVIEW 30s  /  " or "") .. (option.artist or "Unknown artist"), UDim2.new(1,-176,0,18), UDim2.fromOffset(82,29), Enum.Font.Gotham, 8, MUTED, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, 108)

        local heart = makeIconButton(row, "♡", UDim2.fromOffset(30,30), UDim2.new(1,-68,0.5,-15), PANEL, MUTED, 110)
        heart.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        heart.TextSize = 15
        local add = makeIconButton(row, "+", UDim2.fromOffset(30,30), UDim2.new(1,-34,0.5,-15), PANEL, MUTED, 110)
        add.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        add.TextSize = 14

        rows[i] = {
            row=row, playArea=playArea, index=indexLabel, cover=cover, fallback=fallback,
            title=title, artist=artist, heart=heart, add=add, selectedBar=selectedBar, rowStroke=rowStroke
        }

        row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            row.BackgroundColor3 = FearMusic.index == i and Color3.fromRGB(32,32,37) or Color3.fromRGB(20,20,24)
        end)
        row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            local selected = FearMusic.index == i
            row.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1
            row.BackgroundColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(32,32,37) or PANEL
        end)

        playArea.Activated:Connect(function()
            FearMusic.index = i
            _fearPlaySong(i, true)
            if refreshRows then refreshRows() end
            requestCover(i)
        end)

        heart.Activated:Connect(function()
            local key = _fearSongKey(option)
            FearMusic.favorites[key] = FearMusic.favorites[key] ~= true
            _fearMusicSave()
            if refreshRows then refreshRows() end
            _fearRefreshMusicUI()
        end)

        add.Activated:Connect(function()
            local key = _fearSongKey(option)
            FearMusic.playlist[key] = FearMusic.playlist[key] ~= true
            _fearMusicSave()
            if refreshRows then refreshRows() end
        end)
    end

    refreshNav = function()
        for view, data in pairs(navButtons) do
            local selected = FearMusic.view == view
            data.button.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1
            data.button.BackgroundColor3 = selected and CARD or BLACK
            data.text.TextColor3 = selected and WHITE2 or MUTED
            data.icon.TextColor3 = selected and GREEN or MUTED
        end
        local likedCount = countMap(FearMusic.favorites)
        local playlistCount = countMap(FearMusic.playlist)
        likedCountLabel.Text = tostring(likedCount) .. (likedCount == 1 and " song" or " songs")
        playlistCountLabel.Text = "Playlist · " .. tostring(playlistCount)
    end

    refreshRows = function()
        local query = string.lower(search.Text or "")
        local visibleCount = 0
        local likedCount = countMap(FearMusic.favorites)
        local playlistCount = countMap(FearMusic.playlist)

        if FearMusic.view == "liked" then
            viewTitle.Text = "Liked Songs"
            viewSub.Text = tostring(likedCount) .. (likedCount == 1 and " song" or " songs")
        elseif FearMusic.view == "playlist" then
            viewTitle.Text = "My Playlist"
            viewSub.Text = tostring(playlistCount) .. (playlistCount == 1 and " song" or " songs")
        else
            viewTitle.Text = "All Songs"
            viewSub.Text = tostring(#M4LWARE_JUKEBOX) .. " songs"
        end

        for i, option in ipairs(M4LWARE_JUKEBOX) do
            local key = _fearSongKey(option)
            local allowed = FearMusic.view == "all"
                or (FearMusic.view == "liked" and FearMusic.favorites[key] == true)
                or (FearMusic.view == "playlist" and FearMusic.playlist[key] == true)

            local hay = string.lower((option.name or "") .. " " .. (option.artist or "") .. " " .. (option.aliases or ""))
            local match = query == "" or string.find(hay, query, 1, true) ~= nil
            local visible = allowed and match
            local entry = rows[i]
            entry.row.Visible = visible

            if visible then
                visibleCount = visibleCount + 1
                entry.index.Text = tostring(visibleCount)
                requestCover(i)
            end

            local selected = FearMusic.index == i
            entry.row.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1
            entry.row.BackgroundColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(32,32,37) or PANEL
            entry.selectedBar.Visible = selected
            entry.index.Text = selected and "▶" or (visible and tostring(visibleCount) or tostring(i))
            entry.title.TextColor3 = selected and GREEN or WHITE2
            entry.artist.TextColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(205,208,214) or MUTED
            entry.index.TextColor3 = selected and GREEN or MUTED2
            entry.heart.Text = FearMusic.favorites[key] == true and "♥" or "♡"
            entry.heart.TextColor3 = FearMusic.favorites[key] == true and GREEN or MUTED
            entry.add.Text = FearMusic.playlist[key] == true and "✓" or "+"
            entry.add.TextColor3 = FearMusic.playlist[key] == true and GREEN or MUTED
        end

        refreshNav()
    end

    local function refreshSongsTheme()
        local songWhite = getThemeColor()
        SunUI.trackSurface(app)
        SunUI.applySurface(app)
        GREEN = songWhite
        app.BackgroundColor3 = SunUI.palette.base
        sidebar.BackgroundColor3 = SunUI.palette.panel
        if FearMusic.scene then SunUI.tintScene(FearMusic.scene, songWhite) end

        appStroke.Color = songWhite
        playlistStroke.Color = songWhite
        searchStroke.Color = songWhite
        sizeInputStroke.Color = songWhite
        headerLine.BackgroundColor3 = songWhite
        bottomLine.BackgroundColor3 = songWhite
        progressFill.BackgroundColor3 = songWhite
        list.ScrollBarImageColor3 = songWhite
        brandDot.BackgroundColor3 = songWhite

        for _, entry in ipairs(rows) do
            if entry.rowStroke then entry.rowStroke.Color = songWhite end
            if entry.selectedBar then entry.selectedBar.BackgroundColor3 = songWhite end
        end
    end

    _G.M4RefreshMusicTheme = refreshSongsTheme
    refreshSongsTheme()

    local function setView(view)
        if view ~= "all" and view ~= "liked" and view ~= "playlist" then view = "all" end
        FearMusic.view = view
        search.Text = ""
        _fearMusicSave()
        refreshRows()
    end

    allNav.Activated:Connect(function() setView("all") end)
    likedNav.Activated:Connect(function() setView("liked") end)
    playlistNav.Activated:Connect(function() setView("playlist") end)
    playlistCard.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            setView("playlist")
        end
    end)
    search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(refreshRows)

    _fearRefreshMusicUI = function()
        local option = M4LWARE_JUKEBOX[FearMusic.index]
        nowTitle.Text = FearMusic.loading and "Loading..." or (option and (option.name .. (option.preview and "  [PREVIEW 30s]" or "")) or "Choose a song")
        nowArtist.Text = option and (option.artist or "Unknown artist") or "Sunburn Songs"
        playBtn.Text = FearMusic.playing and "Ⅱ" or "▶"
        FearMusic.looped = false
        volumeLbl.Text = tostring(math.floor((FearMusic.volume / 1.5) * 150 + 0.5)) .. "%"

        if option then
            local key = _fearSongKey(option)
            local liked = FearMusic.favorites[key] == true
            nowHeart.Text = liked and "♥" or "♡"
            nowHeart.TextColor3 = liked and GREEN or MUTED
            local cached = _fearCoverAssets[key]
            setCover(nowCover, nowFallback, cached)
            requestCover(FearMusic.index)
        else
            nowHeart.Text = "♡"
            nowHeart.TextColor3 = MUTED
            setCover(nowCover, nowFallback, nil)
        end

        if FearMusic.sound then
            FearMusic.sound.Volume = FearMusic.volume
            FearMusic.sound.Looped = false
        end
        if refreshRows then refreshRows() end
    end

    nowHeart.Activated:Connect(function()
        local option = M4LWARE_JUKEBOX[FearMusic.index]
        if not option then return end
        local key = _fearSongKey(option)
        FearMusic.favorites[key] = FearMusic.favorites[key] ~= true
        _fearMusicSave()
        _fearRefreshMusicUI()
    end)

    playBtn.Activated:Connect(_fearTogglePlay)
    prevBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
        local previousIndex = _fearAdjacentSongInView(FearMusic.index, -1, FearMusic.view)
        if previousIndex then _fearPlaySong(previousIndex, true) end
    end)
    nextBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
        local nextIndex = _fearRandomSongInView(FearMusic.view, FearMusic.index)
        if nextIndex then _fearPlaySong(nextIndex, true) end
    end)

    volMinus.Activated:Connect(function()
        FearMusic.volume = math.clamp(FearMusic.volume - 0.1, 0, 1.5)
        if FearMusic.sound then FearMusic.sound.Volume = FearMusic.volume end
        _fearMusicSave()
        _fearRefreshMusicUI()
    end)
    volPlus.Activated:Connect(function()
        FearMusic.volume = math.clamp(FearMusic.volume + 0.1, 0, 1.5)
        if FearMusic.sound then FearMusic.sound.Volume = FearMusic.volume end
        _fearMusicSave()
        _fearRefreshMusicUI()
    end)

    progressHit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local sound = FearMusic.sound
        if not sound then return end
        local length = 0
        pcall(function() length = sound.TimeLength end)
        if length <= 0 then return end
        local w = math.max(1, progressBack.AbsoluteSize.X)
        local ratio = math.clamp((input.Position.X - progressBack.AbsolutePosition.X) / w, 0, 1)
        pcall(function() sound.TimePosition = ratio * length end)
    end)

    local function applySizeLevel(value, saveNow)
        local numberValue = tonumber(value)
        if not numberValue then
            sizeInput.Text = tostring(FearMusic.musicSizeLevel or 5)
            return
        end

        local level = math.clamp(math.floor(numberValue + 0.5), 1, 10)
        FearMusic.musicSizeLevel = level
        FearMusic.playerScale = 0.5 + (level * 0.1)
        FearMusic.libraryScale = FearMusic.playerScale
        sizeInput.Text = tostring(level)
        applyMusicScale()

        if saveNow ~= false then
            _fearMusicSave()
        end
    end

    sizeInput.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            task.defer(function()
                if sizeInput and sizeInput.Parent then sizeInput:CaptureFocus() end
            end)
        end
    end)

    sizeInput.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        applySizeLevel(sizeInput.Text, true)
    end)

    sizeInput:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        local cleaned = tostring(sizeInput.Text or ""):gsub("[^0-9]", "")
        if #cleaned > 2 then cleaned = cleaned:sub(1, 2) end
        if cleaned ~= sizeInput.Text then
            sizeInput.Text = cleaned
        end
    end)

    local function closeMusicUI()
        app.Visible = false
        sizeInput:ReleaseFocus(false)
    end
    close.Activated:Connect(closeMusicUI)
    close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(closeMusicUI)

    local dragArea = Instance.new("Frame", app)
    dragArea.Size = UDim2.new(1, -132, 0, 58)
    dragArea.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    dragArea.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    dragArea.Active = true
    dragArea.ZIndex = 103

    local dragging = false
    local dragStart = nil
    local appStart = nil
    dragArea.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            appStart = app.Position
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and dragStart and appStart and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            local s = math.max(0.01, appScale.Scale)
            app.Position = UDim2.new(appStart.X.Scale, appStart.X.Offset + delta.X / s, appStart.Y.Scale, appStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y / s)
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)

    _songState._openMusicPlayer = function()
        if not gui.Parent or not app.Parent then return false end
        app.Visible = true
        refreshRows()
        _fearRefreshMusicUI()
        return true
    end
    gui.Destroying:Connect(_fearDestroySound)

    refreshRows()
    _fearRefreshMusicUI()

    task.spawn(function()
        local priority = {FearMusic.index}
        for i = 1, #M4LWARE_JUKEBOX do
            if i ~= FearMusic.index then table.insert(priority, i) end
        end
        for _, i in ipairs(priority) do
            if not gui.Parent then return end
            requestCover(i)
            task.wait(0.16)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while gui.Parent do
            local sound = FearMusic.sound
            local position, length = 0, 0
            if sound then
                pcall(function() position = sound.TimePosition end)
                pcall(function() length = sound.TimeLength end)
                pcall(function() FearMusic.playing = sound.Playing end)
            end
            timeNow.Text = _fearFormatTime(position)
            timeTotal.Text = _fearFormatTime(length)
            local ratio = length > 0 and math.clamp(position / length, 0, 1) or 0
            progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(ratio,0,1,0)
            playBtn.Text = FearMusic.playing and "Ⅱ" or "▶"
            task.wait(0.10)
        end
    end)
end

_fearBuildMusicUI()
return _songState
end
function SunUI.openSongs()
    if SunUI.songsOpening then return end
    if SunUI.songsController and type(SunUI.songsController._openMusicPlayer) == "function" then
        local ok, opened = pcall(SunUI.songsController._openMusicPlayer)
        if ok and opened then return end
        if not ok then warn("[Sunburn Songs] reopen: " .. tostring(opened)) end
    end
    SunUI.songsOpening = true
    local ok, result = pcall(SunUI.createSongs)
    SunUI.songsOpening = false
    if ok then
        SunUI.songsController = result
        print("[Sunburn Songs] abierto")
    else
        warn("[Sunburn Songs] " .. tostring(result))
    end
end


-- Open Songs from S11 hub: add to FeatureToggles and a mobile/settings action

-- ========== SUNBURN SONGS INTEGRATION ==========
pcall(function()
    FeatureToggles = FeatureToggles or {}
    FeatureToggles["Sunburn Songs"] = function()
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                if type(SunUI) == "table" and type(SunUI.openSongs) == "function" then
                    SunUI.openSongs()
                else
                    warn("[SUNBURN Songs] openSongs no disponible")
                end
            end)
            if not ok then warn("[SUNBURN Songs] Error: " .. tostring(err)) end
        end)
    end
    _G.SunburnOpenSongs = FeatureToggles["Sunburn Songs"]
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    for attempt = 1, 50 do
        task.wait(0.2)
        local done = false
        pcall(function()
            local coreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
            local hub = coreGui:FindFirstChild("S11Hub")
            if not hub and LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
                hub = LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("S11Hub")
            end
            if not hub then return end

            local fab = hub:FindFirstChild("OpenSongsFab")
            if fab then fab:Destroy() end

            if hub:FindFirstChild("SunburnSongsConfigBtn", true) then
                done = true
                return
            end

            -- Prefer the CONFIG / Mobile scrolling page
            local parent = nil
            for _, d in ipairs(hub:GetDescendants()) do
                if (d:IsA("ScrollingFrame") or d:IsA("Frame")) then
                    local n = string.lower(d.Name or "")
                    if n == "mobile" or n:find("config", 1, true) or n:find("settings", 1, true) then
                        parent = d
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if not parent then
                for _, d in ipairs(hub:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local t = string.lower(d.Text or "")
                        if t:find("hide gui", 1, true) or t:find("gui size", 1, true) or t:find("show buttons", 1, true) then
                            parent = d.Parent and d.Parent.Parent
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if not parent then return end

            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Name = "SunburnSongsConfigBtn"
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 28, 8)
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.LayoutOrder = 50
            row.ZIndex = 15
            row.Parent = parent
            Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -8)
            btn.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 4)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 48, 10)
            btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            btn.Text = "♫  Sunburn Songs"
            btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
            btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            btn.TextSize = 13
            btn.AutoButtonColor = true
            btn.ZIndex = 16
            btn.Parent = row
            Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
            st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 0)
            st.Thickness = 1.2

            local function openIt()
                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                    if type(SunUI) == "table" and type(SunUI.openSongs) == "function" then
                        SunUI.openSongs()
                    else
                        error("SunUI.openSongs missing")
                    end
                end)
                if not ok then
                    warn("[SUNBURN Songs] " .. tostring(err))
                end
            end
            btn.Activated:Connect(openIt)
            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(openIt)
            done = true
        end)
        if done then break end
    end
end)
